tfw_game_start = {
	tfw_history_start = yes
}

tfw_history_start = {
	title:k_forochel = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:e_annuvorodwaith
		}
		title:k_grey_foothills = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:e_iron_mountains
		}
		title:k_angmar = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:e_annuvorodwaith
		}
		
		title:k_emyn_nimbrith = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:e_misty_mountains
		}
		title:d_foryagsh = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:k_emyn_nimbrith
		}
		title:d_haryagsh = {
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:k_emyn_nimbrith
		}
		title:d_kanelemas = {
			every_de_jure_county = {
				limit = {
					any_county_province = {
						terrain = halls
					}
				}
				set_de_jure_liege_title = title:d_emyn_nimbrith
			}
		}
}

tfw_building_start = {
	every_county_in_region = {
		region = middleearth_forodwaith
		limit = {
			is_wastelands = no
		}
		title_province = {
			generate_building = yes
		}
	}
}

tfw_elven_homelands_start = {
	title:e_tormequendi = {
		every_de_jure_county = {
			add_county_modifier = elven_homeland_county_modifier
		}
	}
	title:e_frostwood = {
		every_de_jure_county = {
			add_county_modifier = elven_homeland_county_modifier
		}
	}
}

tfw_frozen_waste_monthly_tick = {
	set_variable = { name = progress_points value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			title:c_aurora_isles_2.holder = {
				faith_is_good = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_county_in_region = {
					region = middleearth_forodwaith_angband
					holder = character:tfw_lineofnixni_001
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			global_var:tfw_angband_cleansed = 1
		}
		change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			global_var:tfw_frostwood_cleansed = 1
		}
		change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:progress_points = 100
		}
		every_situation_participant = {
			trigger_event = tfw_situation.0005
		}
		end_situation = yes
	}
}

tfw_trigger_intro_events = {

	character:tfw_lineofadlantrim_mornaer_001 = { # Talanthir
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = tfw_intro.001
				days = 1
			}
			lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = intro_event }
		}
	}

	character:tfw_lineoffeanor_001 = { # Evermist
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = tfw_intro.002
				days = 1
			}
			lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = intro_event }
		}
	}

	character:tfw_lineofheorwulf_001 = { # Graw
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = tfw_story.001
				days = 1
			}
			lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = story_content }
		}
	}
	
	character:tfw_lineofsindri_002 = { # Eitri
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = tfw_intro.005
				days = 1
			}
			lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = story_content }
		}
	}
	character:tfw_lineofnixni_001 = { # Nixni
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = tfw_intro.006
				days = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date = 6787.1.1
				}
				lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = story_content }
			}
			else = {
				lotr_set_lore_character_for_bookmark = { TYPE_OF_CONTENT = intro_event }
			}
		}
	}
}


tfw_make_halls_wastelands = {
	# If surface dwellers **somehow** manage to get underground terrain, make it wilderness ( and give them a bit of gold in return )
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:title ?= {
				tier = tier_county
				title_province ?= { terrain = halls }
			}
			NOR = {
				is_dwarf = yes
				is_orc = yes
				is_nazgul = yes
				is_maiar_fallen = yes
				is_elf = yes
			}
		}
		make_settlement_county_wilderness = { COUNTY = scope:title }
		add_gold = 125
		debug_log = "Wilderness: Surface Race got Underground - Making Wasteland"
	}
}

create_artifact_maegdaes_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = maegdaes_name
		description = {
			desc = maegdaes_description
			
		}
		type = sword
		visuals = orcrist
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created
			date = 118.1.1
			recipient = character:lineoffeanor10 #Turgon
		}
		modifier = orcrist_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
		equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
		set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
		set_variable = orcrist
		set_variable = elven
		elven_artifact_reclaimed_effect = yes
		set_variable = relic 
		save_scope_as = epic
		
	}
}

claim_tol_faron = {
	hidden_effect = {
		title:k_tol_faron = {
			every_de_jure_county = {
				
				change_development_level = 1
				
				save_temporary_scope_as = target_county
						
				ai_colonisation_effect = { WASTELANDS = scope:target_county ROOT_SCOPE = root }
						
				scope:target_county = {
					if = {
						limit = { root = { is_elf = yes } }
						title_province = { set_holding_type = elven_holding }
					} else_if = {
						limit = { title_province = { terrain = halls } }
						title_province = { set_holding_type = dwarven_holding }
					} else_if = {
						limit = { root = { is_hobbit = yes } }
						title_province = { set_holding_type = city_holding }
					} else = {
						if = {
							limit = { root = { has_government = tribal_government } }
							title_province = { set_holding_type = tribal_holding }
						} else = {
							title_province = { set_holding_type = castle_holding }
						}
					}
				}		
		
				if = { #Fallback
					limit = { faith = faith:faith_wastelands_pagan }
					set_county_faith = root.faith
				}
				if = { #Fallback
					limit = { culture = culture:wastelands }
					set_county_culture = root.culture
				}
				generate_coa = yes
			}
			
		}
		root = {
			change_government = feudal_government
			get_title = title:k_tol_faron
			get_title = title:d_tol_faron
			set_realm_capital = title:b_tol_feredir_4
			current_travel_plan = {
				abort_travel_plan = yes
			}
			set_location_to_default = yes
			
		}
		
		
		
	}
	custom_tooltip = claim_tol_faron
}

# BEGIN RESTORATION: common/scripted_effects/tfw_orc_restoration_effects.txt
# Initialize only numeric state; milestone flags exist only when completed.
# Safe on existing saves: never resets funding or completion milestones.
tfw_restoration_initialize_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = tfw_restoration_prayer_piety } }
		set_global_variable = { name = tfw_restoration_prayer_piety value = 0 }
	}
}

# Situation scope. Progress uses milestone flags and a native independent-ruler query.
tfw_orc_restoration_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = tfw_orc_restoration_completed } }
		save_scope_as = tfw_restoration_situation
		set_variable = { name = progress_points value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { tfw_restoration_angband_cleansed = yes }
			change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { sauron_is_alive = no }
			change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = tfw_restoration_prayer_completed }
			change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tfw_restoration_no_evil_orc_rulers = yes }
			change_variable = { name = progress_points add = 25 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:progress_points = 100 }
			set_global_variable = { name = tfw_orc_restoration_completed value = yes }
			culture:tfw_restored_adlantrim = {
				get_all_innovations_from = culture:adlantrim
				reset_culture_creation_date = yes
			}
			# One dispatch pass. Conversion is performed by each recipient's event.
			every_living_character = {
				if = {
					limit = { tfw_restoration_good_orc = yes }
					trigger_event = tfw_situation.0007
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_ruler = yes }
					trigger_event = tfw_situation.0006
				}
			}
			add_elven_hope_global_effect = { VALUE = 1000 HOPE_EFFECT = effect_elven_hope_good_realm_rises }
			end_situation = yes
		}
	}
}

# Character scope. Called by the recipient's conversion event, not by a world scan.
# Culture change makes repeat delivery harmless because is_orc becomes false.
tfw_restore_good_orcs_to_elves = {
    if = {
        limit = { tfw_restoration_good_orc = yes }
        culture = { save_scope_as = tfw_restoration_source_culture }
        culture:tfw_restored_adlantrim = {
            get_all_innovations_from = scope:tfw_restoration_source_culture
        }
        set_culture = culture:tfw_restored_adlantrim
        set_ethnicity = sindar_ethnicity
        every_held_title = {
            limit = { tier = tier_county }
            set_county_culture = culture:tfw_restored_adlantrim
        }
        every_held_title = {
            limit = { tier = tier_barony }
            title_province = {
                if = {
                    limit = { has_holding = yes NOT = { has_holding_type = wastelands_holding } }
                    tfw_restoration_convert_province = yes
                }
            }
        }
        # Buildings precede government on-actions; culture establishes eligibility.
        if = { limit = { is_landed = yes } change_government = elven_government }
    }
}
